### listPerformances

**`GET /events/{eventId}/performances`**: List performances of an event

**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`). What that means (ADAM CR-305 G1, option A, Chinmay, 29 October 2026; CHG-CR305-001): a guest or a visitor sees a performance whose `status` is `onSale` or `soldOut`, and only when its event is one `listEvents` shows a guest (`onSale` or `live`, and `isActive`); for any other event the path's event answers `404 not-found`. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff see every `status` (ADR-0025).
**Order.** Soonest first, by `startsAt` ascending, then by `id`.
**Statuses.** For staff every `status` is returned, ended ones included (`cancelled`); a screen that shows only live rows filters on `status` itself. For a guest or a visitor only `onSale` and `soldOut`, as above.

### updateEvent

**`PATCH /events/{eventId}`**: Amend an event

**`parentEventId`, the group an event belongs to** (accepted 26 October 2026, Chinmay, ADAM CR-256 option A). It must name an existing event the caller can see, at the same venue; not the event itself; and one level only: a parent has no parent, and an event that is a parent cannot be given one. Otherwise `400 validation` on `parentEventId`. The key `catalogue.event.parent_event_id` -> `catalogue.event` is declared. Sending `null` takes the event out of its group.

## Group: events

### cloneEvent

**`POST /events/{eventId}/clone`**: Copy an event, choosing what comes with it

Event board 1.3. **Most events are last year's event**, and the question is never whether to clone but what to bring: the schedule, the prices, the seat map, the resource plan, the staff roster — rarely all of them, and never the sales.

**What each `include` copies** (ADAM CR-109, option 2, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-009). The copy is a new `catalogue.event` in `draft`, and only Catalogue's own rows are copied:
- `schedule`: the `catalogue.event_schedule` row and every `catalogue.performance` of the source that is not cancelled, each as a new performance in `scheduled`. With `capacityProfile` too, each copied performance gets its `catalogue.channel_capacity` rows with `sold` and `leased` 0 and `remaining` equal to `capacity`.
- `pricing`: the `catalogue.price_assignment` rows whose `event_id` is the source, with `event_id` the copy; the price lists and prices they point at are shared, not copied.
- `capacityProfile`: the `catalogue.event_capacity_profile` rows (the event's, and with `schedule` each copied performance's).
- `seatMap`: the copied capacity profiles keep the source's `seat_map_id`, a reference to Seating's map, never a copy of it (without `seatMap` it is null on the copy).
- `resourceRequirements`: the `catalogue.event_resource_plan` row's requirements and contractors, with nothing secured (`securedCount` 0, no `bookingIds`) and readiness worked out again.
- `registrationForm`: the `catalogue.event_registration` row, naming the same form.
- `staffPlan` and `accessRules` are another module's data (Workforce and Access) and have no route yet; asked for, they are refused `422 include-not-routed` and nothing is copied.
**shiftDatesByDays** moves, by that many days, every copied time: the performances' `starts_at` and `ends_at`, the schedule's performance times, the resource requirements' `from` and `to`, and the price assignments' `effective_from` and `effective_to`. Sales, holds and orders are never copied. An unknown source event, or one outside the caller's scope, is 404.

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.channel_capacity`, `catalogue.event`, `catalogue.event_capacity_profile`, `catalogue.event_registration`, `catalogue.event_resource_plan`, `catalogue.event_schedule`, `catalogue.performance`, `catalogue.price_assignment` |
| Called by | BO-696 |

### setEventLifecycleState

**`POST /events/{eventId}/lifecycle`**: Draft, planned, on sale, live, closed, cancelled

Event boards 7.2 and 7.3. **The transitions are the product.** Going on sale requires a price, a capacity and a schedule; going live requires a resource plan.

**The state model is `states/event.yaml`** (ADAM CR-109, option 2, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-009): the moves allowed, and each precondition against named columns. A move it does not allow is `409 invalid-event-transition`; a move whose preconditions fail is `409 event-preconditions-not-met`, listing each one missing. **Cancelling** is refused `409 event-has-sales` while any performance of the event that is not cancelled has sales (`catalogue.channel_capacity.sold` above 0); cancelling the event cancels none of its performances, and each performance is cancelled with its own treatment through `cancelPerformance` (the approved screen has only Move to, Reason and Effective from, so this body carries no treatment). Each move writes `event.lifecycleChanged` to the outbox in the same transaction.

**effectiveFrom.** Null or not in the future: the move is made now. In the future: the move is checked now, kept on the event (`scheduledLifecycleState`, `scheduledLifecycleAt`, `scheduledLifecycleReason`; a later request replaces it), and applied at that time by the event lifecycle job in the workers host, which checks the preconditions again and, if they no longer hold, clears the scheduled move and changes nothing else. The 200 answers the event as it is after the request.

**Approval routing above a configured impact waits for its threshold** (CR-109): nothing routes through `approvals` yet. An unknown event, or one outside the caller's scope, is 404.

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.event`, `catalogue.performance`, `platform.outbox` |
| Called by | BO-716, BO-717 |

## Group: hold

### createSeatHold

**`POST /seat-holds`**: Hold specific seats

Short-lived and explicit, unlike a lease. Leases cover interchangeable units; a seat hold covers named seats and cannot be sub-allocated.
The response carries `expiresAt` so the client can show a countdown. A selection that expires silently while a guest enters card details is the worst outcome in this flow.
Where seating rules apply, holding a seat may implicitly buffer its neighbours — those appear in `bufferedSeatIds`.

**Seats per guest booking is a venue setting** (decided 29 September, rev 3 REV3-7). On a guest channel (Guest Web, Guest App), the seats in this request plus the seats the same guest already holds on the same performance may not exceed `VenueSettings.seating.maxSeatsPerGuestOrder` (default 10, bounds 1 to 50); above it the hold is refused with `422` `seat-limit-exceeded` and nothing is held. Staff and POS sales keep at most 10 seats per sale (audit R080 (c)), refused the same way.
**A guest does this within their own session** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-003; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest needs no permission and acts only for themselves, on their own cart, hold, order or payment in their own session. `ORDER_CREATE` is what a staff caller must hold to do it for a guest at a till or in the back office (ADR-0025).

**The hold model on a guest screen: one hold per tap** (r8 walk of tickets 28202 and 28197). Each tap on a free seat is one `createSeatHold` with that seat, and the screen keeps the `holdId` of each. A deselect is `relinquishSeatHold` of that seat's hold. The seats a guest holds on a performance are therefore several holds, which is what the `seat-limit-exceeded` rule above already counts. **Switching the time** relinquishes the guest's holds on the performance being left, then holds the seats still wanted on the new one, one hold each; a seat the new time does not have free is dropped and the screen says so. The way on to the basket (the edge from WEB-007 or GST-049) carries the list of `holdIds`. A screen opened again with a `holdId` reads it with `getSeatHold`.

| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Lock | rowExclusive |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.seat_reservation` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `platform.outbox`, `seating.seat_reservation` |
| Called by | BO-994, GST-049, POS-004, WEB-007 |
| State model | Seat hold ([states/seat-hold.yaml](../../../states/seat-hold.yaml)): created as `held`<br/>Seat ([states/seat.yaml](../../../states/seat.yaml)): moves `available` -> `held` |

### extendSeatHold

**`POST /seat-holds/{holdId}/extend`**: Extend a hold

For a guest still completing payment. Bounded by the venue's maximum, so a hold cannot be renewed indefinitely against a busy performance.
**The bounds are venue settings with a tenant default** (decided 28 September, audit R094): each extension adds `VenueSettings.seating.seatHoldExtensionSeconds` and a hold may be extended at most `VenueSettings.seating.seatHoldMaxExtensions` times. **Proposed defaults 300 seconds and 2 extensions, client to correct (audit R094).**

**A guest extends their own hold within their own session** (3 October 2026, CHG-R1S-023; as `createSeatHold`, Chinmay, 3 October, Pattern 4 group 3: purchase actions are guest-callable within the guest's own session). WEB-011 offers it from the warning before the hold lapses; a guest needs no permission and extends only a hold of their own cart. `ORDER_CREATE` is what a staff caller holds to do it for a guest.

### getSeatHold

**`GET /seat-holds/{holdId}`**: Read a hold

**A guest may read their own hold** (r8 walk of ticket 28202), so WEB-007 and GST-049, opened again with a `holdId`, show the seats and the time left. On a guest channel only a hold the same guest session or subject placed with `createSeatHold` can be read; any other hold answers `404`, exactly as `relinquishSeatHold` does, so hold ids cannot be probed. Staff keep reading any hold at their venue under `ORDER_VIEW`.

| 404 | NotFound | The resource does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope. |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### relinquishSeatHold

**`DELETE /seat-holds/{holdId}`**: Release a hold

Releases buffered neighbours alongside the held seats.

**A guest may release their own hold** (decided 29 September, readiness close-out; the seat-map time bar on WEB-007 / GST-049 lets a guest give seats back before the hold runs out rather than leaving them locked until `expiresAt`). On a guest channel only a hold the same guest session or subject placed with `createSeatHold` can be released; any other hold answers `404`, as anything outside the caller's scope does, so hold ids cannot be probed. A hold already converted to an order, released or expired answers `409` `hold-not-active`. Staff at a till keep releasing any hold at their venue under `ORDER_CREATE`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `ORDER_CREATE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.seat_reservation` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `platform.outbox`, `seating.seat_reservation` |
| Called by | BO-999, GST-049, POS-004, WEB-007 |
| State model | Seat hold ([states/seat-hold.yaml](../../../states/seat-hold.yaml)): moves `held` -> `released`<br/>Seat ([states/seat.yaml](../../../states/seat.yaml)): moves `held` -> `available` |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| holdId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 204 |  | Released |
| 404 | NotFound | The resource does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope. |
| 409 |  | The hold is no longer active (hold-not-active) - converted to an order, already released or expired. |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |


## Group: lease

### convertInventoryHold

**`POST /inventory-holds/{inventoryHoldId}/convert`**: Turn a paid hold into sold units

**Called by the order service inside the order transaction when the order is paid** (`orders.checkoutCart`, `orders.createPayment`; decided 29 September, SD-023). Moves the hold's `grantedUnits` less anything already reported consumed from `leased` to `sold` on its `catalogue.channel_capacity` row, sets the hold `converted` with the order id, and stops the expiry sweeper from ever returning those units. **Idempotent on `orderId`**: converting a hold already converted for the same order answers `200` with the hold as it is.
**A hold that is no longer `active` is refused `409`** (`hold-not-active`): it expired, was released or force-released, or was converted for another order, so its units may already be sold to someone else. The order service then re-acquires with `acquireInventoryHold` before taking the payment; it never sells against a hold it does not hold. No screen calls this operation.

**The order's seat holds convert in the same call** (3 October 2026, CHG-R1S-010): each held seat of the order moves to `sold` (`seating.seat_reservation`, `seating.seat_reservation_item`) and `seat.sold` is written to the outbox once per seat. Seating belongs to this service, so the seat event is published here and not by the order service that called it.

**Decided 18 October 2026** (ADAM CR-169, option A; CHG-CRA-007). **Seats**: the order's seat reservations are the ones whose `inventoryReservationId` is this hold; they move to `sold` and `seat.sold` is written once per seat. **Envelopes**: where the capacity has channel allocations, the same units move from `leased` to `sold` on the hold's `catalogue.channel_envelope`. **A hold that lapsed after the capture**: the capture stands and the order is paid. The units are taken again if they are still free; otherwise the order is flagged for the reconciler with `refusedReason` `unitsGone`. **Nothing is ever refunded automatically**, PLATFORM-SAGA-PAID's own rule for a dead-lettered issuance. Until the seat reservation's key lands, a seated line is refused with `409 hold-not-active` naming this request.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `None` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `seating.seat_reservation_item` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Lock | rowExclusive |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.channel_capacity`, `catalogue.channel_envelope`, `catalogue.inventory_reservation` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.channel_capacity`, `catalogue.channel_envelope`, `catalogue.inventory_reservation`, `platform.outbox`, `seating.seat_reservation`, `seating.seat_reservation_item` |
| Called by | **no screen**: no screen lists it in its apis, so it is reachable only by API or import until one does (README, Known gaps) |
| State model | Inventory lease ([states/lease.yaml](../../../states/lease.yaml)): moves `active` -> `converted`<br/>Seat hold ([states/seat-hold.yaml](../../../states/seat-hold.yaml)): moves `converted` -> `sold`<br/>Seat ([states/seat.yaml](../../../states/seat.yaml)): moves `held` -> `sold` |

**Parameters**

|---|---|---|---|---|
| inventoryHoldId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| orderId | string (uuid) | yes | The paid order the units are sold to. |
| units | integer |  | Units sold, when fewer than the hold grants (a line reduced before payment). (min 1) |

**Response**: `InventoryHold`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string | yes |  |
| channelCapacityId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| holderKind | InventoryHoldHolderKind: enum (workstation, cart) | yes | Who holds the units (decided 29 September, SD-023). (default workstation) |
| holderWorkstationId | string (uuid) |  | The holding workstation when holderKind is workstation; null on a cart hold, because a browser has none (SD-023, 29 September). (nullable) |
| holderCartId | string (uuid) |  | The holding cart (orders.cart) when holderKind is cart (SD-023, 29 September). (nullable) |
| convertedOrderId | string (uuid) |  | The paid order the hold was converted for, set by convertInventoryHold. (read-only; nullable) |
| convertedAt | string (date-time) |  | (read-only; nullable) |

| releasedAt | string (date-time) |  | (nullable) |
| forceReleasedByPrincipalId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| forceReleaseReason | string |  | (nullable) |

**Responses**

### setPrices

**`PUT /price-lists/{priceListId}/prices`**: Set prices in bulk

Currency must match the region's currency and scale. A price in a currency the region does not use is rejected — money carries its scale, and a mismatched scale silently truncates.
**Accepts `Prefer: validate-only`** (29 September, build): validates and answers 200 with the would-be result without writing, for the AI executor's plan validation.

## Group: product

### checkBookingEligibility

**`POST /eligibility-checks`**: Can this party take part

Checks a party's declared ages and heights against every product in the booking before payment, and says per guest who cannot take part and why, and whether a guardian signature or waiver is needed. **It refuses nothing itself**: checkout refuses a line with an ineligible guest, so the answer here and the refusal there cannot disagree.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.product`, `catalogue.product_eligibility_rule` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency` |
| Called by | GST-007, WEB-006 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `EligibilityCheckRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| productIds | array of string | yes | (min items 1) |
| party | array of EligibilityDeclaration | yes | (min items 1) |
| party[].ageBand | enum (infant, child, junior, adult, senior) |  | Infant under 3, child 3–12, junior 13–17, adult 18–59, senior 60+. |
| party[].ageYears | integer |  | (nullable) |
| party[].heightBandIndex | integer |  | Which band of the rule's heightBandsCm, counting from 0. (nullable) |
| party[].confidentSwimmer | boolean |  | Superseded by the consent record for the product's swim consent question (decided 29 September, rev 3 REV3-26); see ProductEligibilityRule.swimAbility. (nullable) |
| party[].guardianSigned | boolean |  | (default False) |

**Response**: `EligibilityCheckResult`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| eligible | boolean | yes |  |
| guests | array of object |  |  |
| guests[].index | integer |  |  |
| guests[].eligible | boolean |  |  |
| guests[].reasons | array of enum (tooYoung, tooOld, tooShort, tooTall, needsAdult, needsGuardianSignature, needsSwimmer) |  |  |
| waiverRequired | boolean |  |  |

### getProduct

**`GET /products/{productId}`**: Read a product

**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest or a visitor gets a product that is published, and nothing in draft or unpublished; asked for by id, such a record answers as not found. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff also see drafts (ADR-0025).
**One exception: a withdrawn product is returned to a guest or visitor** (decided 20 October 2026, Chinmay, R8 blocker cause GPS-C07, option A; CHG-R8SPN-023), so WEB-004's approved withdrawn banner can show. It comes in the same guest projection, with `lifecycleState` `withdrawn` and `isSellable` false, and only when it was once published; a draft or unpublished product is still not found. Nothing else widens: `listProducts` and the other lists leave withdrawn products out for a guest, and `addCartLine` still refuses one.

| Read routing | replica |
| Guest callable | True |
| Offline note | 24 August: servable from a local cache. |
| Reads | `catalogue.product`, `catalogue.product_media` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-007, BO-008, BO-012, BO-014, EMP-034, GST-004, GST-006, POS-002, POS-003, PTR-006, WEB-004, WEB-006, WEB-022 |

### getProductEligibilityRule

**`GET /products/{productId}/eligibility-rule`**: Who may take part: age, height, supervision

**A product with no rule set answers `200`, not `404`.** The schema's own reading of absence is "anyone may take part", so an unset rule comes back with every limit null and every other field at its default — the body a rule that limits nothing would return. `404` means the product itself is unknown or outside the caller's scope.

### listProductVariants

**`GET /products/{productId}/variants`**: List generated variants

**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest or a visitor gets the published variants of a published product, and nothing in draft, unpublished or withdrawn; asked for by id, such a record answers as not found. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff also see drafts (ADR-0025).
**Each variant carries its list price** (20 October 2026, R8 blocker cause GPS-C27; CHG-R8SPN-028): `listPrice` is the price the guest listing shows for the variant on the caller's channel at this moment, in the venue's currency, resolved by the same price-list rule as `searchCatalogue`'s `fromPrice` and the `variantListingFacts` query's `listPrice`. It is what GST-008's stepper, GST-050, GST-075's price per length and WEB-005 show. It is the list price only: the total a guest pays is still decided at the basket by `promotions.evaluatePromotions` (offers, bundles) and tax is shown as the listing shows it. The channel is the caller's (`web` for the storefront, `mobile` for the guest app, from the credential or, for a visitor, the `channel` parameter); null where no price list prices the variant on that channel.
**Order.** Sorted by `name` ascending, then by `id`.
Where `name` is null the row sorts after the rows that have it (NULLS LAST), and `id` breaks every tie. (r10 default ruling, CHG-R10GEN-004)

|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Guest callable | True |
| Offline note | 24 August: servable from a local cache. |
| Reads | `catalogue.variant` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-007, BO-008, BO-012, BO-048, EMP-034, GST-008, GST-041, GST-048, GST-050, GST-075, KSK-004, KSK-015, POS-002, POS-003, PTR-006, WEB-005, WEB-048 |

|---|---|---|---|---|

| channel | query |  | Channel: enum (pos, kiosk, web, mobile, b2b, ota, callCentre) | The channel listPrice is priced for (GPS-C27, CHG-R8SPN-028). |
| pageSize | query |  | integer |  |
| cursor | query |  | string | Opaque cursor: the nextCursor of the previous page. |
| X-Venue-Id | header |  | string (uuid) | The venue a guest app or site is browsing, named on every public read of venue data (decided 25 October 2026, Chinmay, ADAM CR-198 option A; CHG-R10ID-010; the rule is on guestAuth). |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

| items[].id | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| items[].listPrice | Money |  | The list price on the caller's channel now, resolved on read from the price lists (listProductVariants; R8 blocker cause GPS-C27, CHG-R8SPN-028). (read-only; nullable) |

### listProducts

**`GET /products`**: List products

For guest, partner and back-office callers. **A point-of-sale terminal does not call this on the server** — on a till it is answered from the local catalogue bundle (`getLatestBundle`, ADR-0013), which is what `x-ticvai-offline-capable` means here. A till screen that lists this operation is reading the bundle.
**What a guest caller gets** (decided 29 September, rev 3 REV3-14). Products with `guestListing: bookable` that are on sale at the caller's channel, plus products with `guestListing: infoOnly` whose lifecycle is live, whether or not they are on sale, each carrying its `guestListing` and `notBookableLabel` so the screen shows the label and opens details instead of adding to the basket. `hidden` products are never returned to a guest, and `infoOnly` ones are left out when the venue's `BookingFlowConfig.showInfoOnly` is off. Staff and partner callers see every product, whatever its `guestListing`.
**Category and tag filters** (decided 29 September, rev 3 REV3-16 and REV3-19). `categoryId` narrows to one category of the `listProductCategories` tree, so a guest can go from a category tile to that category's tickets; `segmentTag` narrows by tag, e.g. a surf level.
**Help me choose filters the list** (decided 29 September, W4). `guidedAnswerIds` applies the filter of each chosen answer on the server; the guest screen offers *Show everything*, which is the same call without it. **A partner caller reads here** what is assigned to its channel (M17-04): partners never write products, prices or performances.
**Only published answers filter here; a draft is previewed elsewhere** (decided 20 October 2026, Chinmay, R8 blocker cause WHITE-C26, server option; CHG-R8SPN-036). CMS-101's live preview of a draft guided choice calls white-label's `previewGuidedChoice` with the draft answers, which runs this operation's own answer filter (the same code: age ranges, certification, swimmer, segment tags) over the venue's published products and returns what is left for each combination; the browser never re-implements the filter.
**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest or a visitor gets products that are published and on sale, and nothing in draft, unpublished or withdrawn; asked for by id, such a record answers as not found. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff also see drafts (ADR-0025).


**Order.** Sorted by `displayOrder` ascending, then by `id`.

### resolveProductByCode

**`GET /products/resolve`**: Resolve a partner code to a product

Called on inbound distribution orders. Accepts a partner's own SKU and returns the variant it maps to.

**Which partner, and which variant** (ADAM CR-111, option 1, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-011). A partner caller resolves its own codes: `partnerId` is its own, and one naming another partner is 403. A staff caller must send `partnerId` (400 `partner-required` without it), so a code two partners share is never guessed. A code mapped to a variant returns that variant. A product-level code (the row's `variant_id` null) returns the product's default variant (`isDefault`), or `409 no-default-variant` when the product has none. A code that matches nothing for the partner is 404.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Reads | `catalogue.variant` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-007, BO-012, EMP-034, POS-002, POS-003, PTR-006 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| code | query | yes | string |  |
| partnerId | query |  | string (uuid) | Required from a staff caller (400 partner-required); a partner caller's own, which it may omit (ADAM CR-111). |

| referencePrice | Money |  | The price a saving is measured against (accepted 26 October 2026, Chinmay, ADAM CR-253 option C), set by the back office on the variant (updateProductVariant). (nullable) |
| savingPercent | integer |  | Computed by the server, never in the browser (ADAM CR-253): with a referencePrice and a listPrice below it, the whole percent the list price saves, floor((referencePrice - listPrice) / referencePrice… (read-only; nullable) |
| productId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| sku | string | yes |  |
| axisValues | object | yes |  |
| name | string |  | Taken from their variant tables, 20 September. (max length 150; nullable) |
| barcode | string |  | Taken from their variant tables, 20 September. (max length 64; nullable) |
| isDefault | boolean |  | Taken from their variant tables. (default False) |
| isActive | boolean | yes | False when retired. |
| description | object |  | Who this ticket type is for and what it includes, shown behind the (i) on each Adult, Child, Senior or Infant row (decided 29 September, 23SEP-6). (nullable) |

| 409 |  | The code is a product-level code and the product has no default variant (ADAM CR-111). |

### setAlternativeCodes

**`PUT /products/{productId}/alternative-codes`**: Set external identifiers

**What the PUT does.** The body is the product's whole set of alternative codes: a code left out is removed, a code sent is stored as sent, and an empty `codes` removes them all. A code is unique per partner. Every `variantId` must be a variant of this product; one that belongs to another product is a `400`.

**Unique per partner on the index** (ADAM CR-111, option 1, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-011): `catalogue.alternative_code` is unique on `(partner_id, lower(code))` across the tenant. Alternative codes are venue-scoped since r6, so a lookup under row security cannot see another venue's rows; the duplicate is found by the index refusing the insert, answered `409 duplicate-code`, never by a read first.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `catalogue.alternative_code` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.alternative_code` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.alternative_code` |
| Called by | BO-007, BO-012 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| productId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| codes | array of AlternativeCode | yes |  |
| codes[].scopePath | string | yes | Where the row is owned: the path of the scope that wrote it (its venue, or the region or tenant that configured it), set by the server from the caller's scope and never taken from a request. (read-only) |
| codes[].code | string | yes | (max length 128) |
| codes[].partnerId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| codes[].partnerName | string |  |  |
| codes[].variantId | string (uuid) |  |  |
| codes[].note | string |  | (max length 200) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Set |
| 400 |  | A variantId is not a variant of this product, or the body sends one code twice for the same partner. |
| 409 |  | Code already mapped to a different product for that partner |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### setProductChannelTexts

**`PUT /products/{productId}/channel-texts`**: Set the product's texts per sales channel

**Replaces all of the product's channel texts** (CHG-R8PRD-003): a text sent as null, or left out, falls back to the product's name or description. Each text is keyed by language code, and every key must be an enabled language of the tenant (`422 language-not-enabled`). The length limits are per language value.
**The languages are checked on the service route** (ADAM CR-213, option A, decided 25 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-005): `white-label#tenantLanguageRecords` and, before the tenant's first publish, `white-label#tenantConfigRecords` are asked through WhiteLabel's service route, which answers at the tenant root rather than under the caller's row security, so a venue product administrator's save is checked against the tenant's languages before the first publish as after it. The draft configuration itself stays tenant-root only.
**Versioned as `updateProduct` versions the product**, and refused on an archived product (`409 product-archived`). **Audited:** written to `catalogue.audit_entry` (`domain: product`, `action: update`, `category: channelTexts`, the texts before and after).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `catalogue.product_channel_text` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.product_channel_text` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.audit_entry`, `catalogue.product_channel_text`, `catalogue.product_version` |
| Called by | **no screen**: no screen lists it in its apis, so it is reachable only by API or import until one does (README, Known gaps) |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| productId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `ProductChannelTexts`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) |  | The row's key, set by the server; absent while the product has saved nothing. (read-only) |
| productId | string (uuid) |  | From the path. (read-only) |
| posButtonLabel | object |  | The label on the till's sale button (4.10); at most 30 characters per language. (nullable) |
| receiptName | object |  | The product's name as printed on the receipt and the ticket, which may differ from the button label (4.10); at most 80 characters per language. (nullable) |
| partnerDescription | object |  | What resellers see in the B2B portal and through the API (4.10); at most 4000 characters per language. (nullable) |
| webDescription | object |  | The description the website and the guest app show; null uses the product's description (wireframe B1-S06). (nullable) |
| kioskLabel | object |  | The label on the kiosk's product tile (wireframe B1-S06); at most 40 characters per language. (nullable) |
| visitInstructions | object |  | What the guest is told to do on the day (wireframe B1-S06); at most 2000 characters per language. (nullable) |
| termsAndConditions | object |  | The product's own terms (wireframe B1-S06); at most 8000 characters per language. (nullable) |
| enabledLanguages | array of string |  | The tenant's enabled languages, ISO 639-1 codes in the tenant's order: the published set, or the draft's before the first publish (white-label LanguageConfig.languages). (read-only) |
| missingTranslations | array of object |  | Per enabled language of the tenant, the texts above (and name, description for the product's own) set in some language and not in this one. (read-only) |
| missingTranslations[].language | string | yes | (pattern ^[a-z]{2}$) |
| missingTranslations[].fields | array of enum (name, description, posButtonLabel, receiptName, partnerDescription, webDescription, kioskLabel, visitInstructions, …) | yes |  |
| scopePath | string |  | The partition key (ADR-0005). (read-only) |

**Response**: `ProductChannelTexts`

### setProductEligibilityRule

**`PUT /products/{productId}/eligibility-rule`**: Set age, height and supervision limits

**Design gap, 23 September.** The design gates rides, slides and kids' activities on height and age before booking (*Height & age eligibility gate*, *Swim ability & height gate*). The catalogue had no place for the limits; only games, queues and rentals did.

**Declared, then checked at the gate.** The guest declares a band at booking; staff verify on arrival. Whether an ineligible guest is refunded at the gate is a setting, because the design says they are not.

### setProductOwnerForms

**`PUT /products/{productId}/owner-forms`**: Link a product to the forms its sale asks

**Replaces the product's links** (CHG-R8PRD-004, CHG-R8PRD-009): at most one form per `appliesTo`, so a body naming `reservationOwner` twice is `400 validation`. A form that is not a form of the product's venue is `422 unknown-form`, one whose `kind` is not `dataCapture` or `registration` `422 form-kind-not-allowed`, one with no published version `422 form-not-published`. The link names the form, not a version: a sale asks the version published at the time of the sale, and the submission records which.
**Versioned as `updateProduct` versions the product**, and refused on an archived product (`409 product-archived`). **Audited:** written to `catalogue.audit_entry` (`domain: product`, `action: update`, `category: ownerForms`, the links before and after).

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.audit_entry`, `catalogue.product_owner_form`, `catalogue.product_version` |
| Called by | **no screen**: no screen lists it in its apis, so it is reachable only by API or import until one does (README, Known gaps) |

### setProductSalePermissionRules

**`PUT /products/{productId}/sale-permission-rules`**: Set who can sell this product

**Replaces the product's whole rule set** (CHG-R8PRD-005, CHG-R8PRD-009); an empty `rules` returns the product to selling wherever its channels allow.
**What the server refuses.** A `global` rule with a `targetId`, any other rule without one, two rules with one `level` and `targetId` (`400 validation`); a set with a `DENY` and no `ALLOW` (`400 no-allow-rule`) or a `global` `DENY` (`400 global-deny`), either of which sells nowhere (withdraw the product for that); a new or changed rule whose `targetId` is not one `listSalePermissionTargets` offers for the product at its level (`422 unknown-sale-permission-target`); a rule sent unchanged (same `level`, `targetId` and `effect` as a saved one) is kept as it is and not checked again, so a rule naming a principal since deactivated, or a venue outside the editor's grants, does not block other changes; an `ALLOW` whose target is the caller (`principal`) or the caller's selected role (`role`), or a removed or changed `DENY` whose target is either of them (`403 sale-permission-self-grant`: nobody grants themselves the right to sell, by adding an allow or lifting a deny, as an approver is never the submitter); an archived product (`409 product-archived`).
**Takes effect at once, as `updateProduct` does** (CHG-R8PRD-010), in every lifecycle state but `archived`, and is versioned as `updateProduct` versions the product. Whether a change on a live product should wait for approval is an open question for the client; it is not built. **Audited:** written to `catalogue.audit_entry` (`domain: product`, `action: update`, `category: salePermissions`, the rule set before and after).

|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |

| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Config scope | venue |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.product_sale_permission` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.audit_entry`, `catalogue.product_sale_permission`, `catalogue.product_version` |
| Called by | **no screen**: no screen lists it in its apis, so it is reachable only by API or import until one does (README, Known gaps) |

### updateProduct

**`PATCH /products/{productId}`**: Update a product

**Accepts `Prefer: validate-only`** (29 September, build): validates and answers 200 with the would-be result without writing, for the AI executor's plan validation.

**How a product is versioned**, defined here once for every write that says "versioned as `updateProduct`" (ADAM CR-214, option A, decided 25 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-006). Every committed `updateProduct`, and every product sub-resource PUT that says so (`setProductChannelTexts`, `setProductSalePermissionRules`, `setProductClassification`, `setProductOwnerForms`) and each product `bulkUpdateCatalogueProducts` changes, creates one `catalogue.product_version` row: `version` the product's highest plus one, `is_current` moved to it in the same transaction, `published_at` and `published_by_principal_id` the write's time and caller, `content_hash` and `snapshot` as below. **Unless `content_hash` is unchanged**: a write that changes nothing the hash covers creates no version. `content_hash` is the SHA-256 of the product row and its sub-resources (its `catalogue.product_media`, `catalogue.product_channel_text`, `catalogue.product_sale_permission` and `catalogue.product_owner_form` rows) in canonical JSON; `snapshot` is that same JSON, so `restoreProductVersion` brings back the channel texts and sale-permission rules with the product. A validate-only call writes no version.

**updatedAt** (ADAM CR-204, option A, decided 25 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-007): each of those writes also sets `catalogue.product.updated_at` to its time, whether or not a version is created.

|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.product`, `catalogue.product_channel_text`, `catalogue.product_media`, `catalogue.product_owner_form`, `catalogue.product_sale_permission`, `whitelabel.booking_flow` |

### updateProductVariant

**`PATCH /products/{productId}/variants/{variantId}`**: Describe a variant

**Only the words and codes a person reads or scans.** A variant's combination of values (`axisValues`) and its `sku` come from `setProductAttributes` and are not edited here; this sets the `name`, the `description` a guest reads behind the (i) on a ticket-type row (decided 29 September, 23SEP-6), the `barcode`, which variant is the default, and the `referencePrice` a length's saving is measured against (ADAM CR-253; the answer's `savingPercent` is worked out from it).
Setting `isDefault` true clears it on the product's other variants. A retired variant is not edited: `409`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, changes rows of `catalogue.variant` that another operation creates |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.variant` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.variant` |
| Called by | BO-008 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| productId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| variantId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| name | string |  | (max length 150; nullable) |
| description | object |  | (nullable) |
| barcode | string |  | (max length 64; nullable) |
| isDefault | boolean |  |  |
| referencePrice | Money |  | The price the variant's saving is measured against (ProductVariant.referencePrice; ADAM CR-253); null clears it. (nullable) |

**Response**: `ProductVariant`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| listPrice | Money |  | The list price on the caller's channel now, resolved on read from the price lists (listProductVariants; R8 blocker cause GPS-C27, CHG-R8SPN-028). (read-only; nullable) |
| referencePrice | Money |  | The price a saving is measured against (accepted 26 October 2026, Chinmay, ADAM CR-253 option C), set by the back office on the variant (updateProductVariant). (nullable) |
| savingPercent | integer |  | Computed by the server, never in the browser (ADAM CR-253): with a referencePrice and a listPrice below it, the whole percent the list price saves, floor((referencePrice - listPrice) / referencePrice… (read-only; nullable) |
| productId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| sku | string | yes |  |
| axisValues | object | yes |  |
| name | string |  | Taken from their variant tables, 20 September. (max length 150; nullable) |
| barcode | string |  | Taken from their variant tables, 20 September. (max length 64; nullable) |
| isDefault | boolean |  | Taken from their variant tables. (default False) |
| isActive | boolean | yes | False when retired. |
| description | object |  | Who this ticket type is for and what it includes, shown behind the (i) on each Adult, Child, Senior or Infant row (decided 29 September, 23SEP-6). (nullable) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Updated. |
| 400 |  | A description value longer than 300 characters. |
| 403 | Forbidden | Authenticated but not permitted at the requested scope |
| 404 | NotFound | The resource does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope. |
| 409 |  | The variant is retired (isActive false), or is not a variant of this product. |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |


## Group: promotion

### analysePromotionConflicts

**`GET /promotions/{promotionId}/conflicts`**: Analyse stacking against live promotions

Run before publishing. Reports promotions that overlap on product and period, and what the combined discount would be.
**"Near-zero" has a stated minimum** (decided 28 September, audit R096 (5)): a combination is near-zero when the net line price it leaves falls below the venue setting `promotions.nearZeroLinePrice` (a tenant default the venue may override; proposed AED 1.00, client to correct under audit R094). It is flagged with `isNearZero` for a person to look at. Only zero or below blocks publication (`isBlocking`, audit R101).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Reads | `promotions.promotion` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-010, POS-002 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |

**Response**: `ConflictAnalysis`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| conflicts | array of object | yes |  |
| conflicts[].otherPromotionId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| conflicts[].otherPromotionCode | string | yes |  |
| conflicts[].overlap | enum (products, period, channel, full) | yes |  |
| conflicts[].combinedDiscount | number | yes | Combined percentage where both apply to the same line. |
| conflicts[].isBlocking | boolean |  | True where the combination would produce a line price of zero or below (decided 28 September, audit R101). |
| conflicts[].isNearZero | boolean |  | True where the combination leaves a net line price above zero but below the venue setting promotions.nearZeroLinePrice (proposed AED 1.00; decided 28 September, audit R096 (5)). |
| worstCaseDiscount | number | yes | Largest combined discount any single line could receive. |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Analysis |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### createPromotion

**`POST /promotions`**: Create a promotion

Created in `draft`. A draft promotion never evaluates — publishing is the act that makes it live, and it appears at terminals with the next catalogue bundle.
**Writes `promotions.promotion` and `promotions.promotion_audit`** in the same transaction, as every promotions configuration change does (entityType `promotion`, eventType `promotionCreated`, promotionVersion 1). No channel publication row exists until publishPromotion (decided 29 September, writers pass).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `promotions.promotion` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit` |
| Called by | BO-010 |
| State model | Promotion ([states/promotion.yaml](../../../states/promotion.yaml)): created as `draft` |

### getPromotionUsage

**`GET /promotions/{promotionId}/usage`**: Redemption count and discount given

Also the enforcement surface for budget caps — a promotion may be configured to stop automatically once a total discount value is reached.
**A budget cap is refused at checkout, not only reported** (decided 28 September, audit R101): once `discountGiven` plus the discount on the order being checked out would exceed `budgetCap`, the promotion no longer applies to that order and `isBudgetExhausted` is true.

### listPromotions

**`GET /promotions`**: List promotions

**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest or a visitor gets live, published promotions, and nothing in draft, unpublished or withdrawn; asked for by id, such a record answers as not found. `PRICE_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff also see drafts (ADR-0025).
**Order.** Newest first, by `publishedAt` descending, then by `id`. **A draft has no `publishedAt`** and sorts after every published promotion, newest first by its creation time, which its `id` carries (a UUIDv7), so `published_at DESC NULLS LAST, id DESC` (ADAM CR-111, option 1, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-011).

| Writes | - |

### publishPromotion

**`POST /promotions/{promotionId}/publish`**: Publish a promotion

Runs conflict analysis first. A promotion that stacks with an existing one to produce a negative or near-zero line price is refused — that combination is discovered at a till otherwise, usually by a guest.
**The floor is zero: a combined line price of zero or below is refused** (decided 28 September, audit R101). A line that stays above zero, however small, publishes. One that falls below the near-zero minimum (`ConflictAnalysis`, audit R096 (5)) publishes too, flagged rather than refused.
**Writes `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` and `promotions.promotion_audit`.** One row per sales channel the promotion runs on goes to `pendingPublication` (created there when the channel has none, resumed from `suspended` when it has), for the channel sync job to take up (states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml); the audit row is eventType `promotionActivated` with the published promotionVersion. The conflicts found here are recorded in `promotions.promotion_conflict` by the conflict detection job, not by this operation (decided 29 September, writers pass).

**From which state** (ADAM CR-111, option 1, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-011): a promotion is published from `draft` (to `scheduled`) or resumed from `paused` (to `live`), as `states/promotion.yaml` says. From any other state (`scheduled`, `live`, `expired`, `ended`) it is `409 invalid-promotion-state`. A paused promotion whose `validTo` has passed is not resumed: `409 promotion-window-ended` (end it instead). An unknown promotion, or one outside the caller's scope, is 404.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `promotions.promotion` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `platform.outbox`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Called by | ADM-141, BO-010 |
| State model | Promotion channel publication ([states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml](../../../states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml)): moves `notAssigned` -> `pendingPublication`, `suspended` -> `pendingPublication`<br/>Promotion ([states/promotion.yaml](../../../states/promotion.yaml)): moves `draft` -> `scheduled`, `paused` -> `live` |

### updatePromotion

**`PATCH /promotions/{promotionId}`**: Amend a promotion

Amending a live promotion changes behaviour mid-sale. Conditions and discount are editable only in draft; a live promotion may be paused, extended or ended, nothing more.

**The terms lock and the refusals** (ADAM CR-111, option 1, accepted 15 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-011). `conditions` and `discount` are editable in `draft` only: sent for a promotion that is `scheduled`, `live` or `paused` they are `409 live-promotion-terms-locked` (the problem type keeps its r9 name for all three), and for one that is `expired` or `ended` every field is `409 invalid-promotion-state`. Pausing is `pausePromotion`'s alone (it takes the reason and suspends the channel rows), so this body no longer carries `isPaused`. The discount cap and unsatisfiable conditions are refused `400` exactly as `createPromotion` refuses them. An unknown promotion, or one outside the caller's scope, is 404.

**The guest-facing texts** (ADAM CR-225 and CR-231, option A, decided 25 October 2026; CHG-R10CAT-012): `nameLocalised`, `descriptionLocalised`, `label` and `venues` are display fields, editable in any state but `expired` and `ended`; each `venues[].venueId` must be a venue of the tenant (`tenancy#scopeRecords`), else `422 unknown-venue`.
**Writes `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` and `promotions.promotion_audit`.** Each channel row of the promotion that is `published` moves to `outOfSync` (states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml) and the sync job pushes the new `version`; the audit row records the changed fields before and after (eventType `promotionEdited`, or `discountChanged`, `eligibilityChanged`, `datesChanged` where only that changed) with the new promotionVersion (decided 29 September, writers pass).

|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, changes rows of `promotions.promotion` that another operation creates |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Called by | BO-010 |
| State model | Promotion channel publication ([states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml](../../../states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml)): moves `published` -> `outOfSync` |

## Group: promotions

### setPromotionVariants

**`PUT /promotions/{promotionId}/variants`**: A/B test two versions against each other

BL-114. **Split by a stable hash of the subject, not at random per request** — a guest who sees 10% on Monday and 15% on Tuesday has learned to wait.
**The `trafficPercent` of all variants must sum to exactly 100; there is no minimum per variant** (decided 28 September, audit R101). A variant at 0 is kept but receives no traffic.
**Writes `promotions.promotion_variant` and `promotions.promotion_audit`** in the same transaction, as every promotions configuration change does (entityType `promotion`, eventType `promotionEdited`) (decided 29 September, writers pass).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `promotions.promotion` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_variant` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit`, `promotions.promotion_variant` |
| Called by | ADM-226, BO-010 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| variants | array of PromotionVariant | yes | (min items 2; max items 4) |
| variants[].scopePath | string | yes | Where the row is owned: the path of the scope that wrote it (its venue, or the region or tenant that configured it), set by the server from the caller's scope and never taken from a request. (read-only) |
| variants[].id | string (uuid) |  | (read-only) |
| variants[].promotionId | string (uuid) |  | Taken from the path. (read-only) |
| variants[].label | string | yes |  |
| variants[].trafficPercent | integer | yes | Share of traffic. (min 0; max 100) |
| variants[].discountPercent | number |  |  |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| variants | array of PromotionVariant | yes |  |
| variants[].scopePath | string | yes | Where the row is owned: the path of the scope that wrote it (its venue, or the region or tenant that configured it), set by the server from the caller's scope and never taken from a request. (read-only) |
| variants[].id | string (uuid) |  | (read-only) |
| variants[].promotionId | string (uuid) |  | Taken from the path. (read-only) |

| variants[].trafficPercent | integer | yes | Share of traffic. (min 0; max 100) |
| variants[].discountPercent | number |  |  |

## Group: recommendation

### recommendSeats

**`POST /performances/{performanceId}/seat-recommendations`**: Recommend seats for a party

Best available, best value, closest to stage, or accessible with companions.
Returns contiguous groups where the party requires them — a family of four split across two rows is a complaint, not a booking.
**A guest does this within their own session** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-003; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest needs no permission and acts only for themselves, on their own cart, hold, order or payment in their own session. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold to do it for a guest at a till or in the back office (ADR-0025).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.performance`, `seating.accessible`, `seating.recommendation_rule`, `seating.seat`, `seating.seat_block_item`, `seating.seat_category`, `seating.seat_price_band`, `seating.seat_reservation`, `seating.seat_reservation_item` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency` |
| Called by | BO-015, BO-019, BO-1001, BO-1002, BO-1039, GST-049, WEB-007 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| performanceId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `SeatRecommendationRequest`

|---|---|---|---|

| categoryIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| maxPrice | Money |  | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| maxPrice.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| maxPrice.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |

| options[].displayLabels | array of string |  |  |

## Group: seatMap

### editSeatMapStructure

**`PATCH /seat-maps/{seatMapId}/structure`**: Build a seat map by hand

**The manual seat map building the minutes promised** (MoM 21 Aug 2026; 12 October 2026, CHG-R6-035). BO-954 Venue Canvas and BO-956 Rows & Seats draw sections, rows and seats, but `createSeatMap` names an empty map, `updateSeatMap` saves its name and views and `updateSeats` changes only the category, attribute and status of seats that already exist: a venue with no drawing or manifest to import could not build a map at all. This adds, removes and renumbers the structure of a **draft** map.

**One request, applied in order**: `addSections`, then `addRows` (a row of `seatCount` seats laid out straight or on a curve and numbered from `firstSeatNumber` in `numberingDirection`), then `addSeats` (single seats, for a box or an irregular row), then `removeSeatIds`, then `relabel` (a section code, a row label or a seat number changed). A label already taken in the map, or a removal of a seat on a published map, is refused `409` with the conflicting labels; nothing is applied. **Accepts `Prefer: validate-only`**, as `updateSeats` does, for the AI executor's plan validation (`proposeSeatMapChanges`, kind `numbering`).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `CAPACITY_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, changes rows of `seating.seat` that another operation creates |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.seat_map` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.seat`, `seating.seat_map`, `seating.section` |
| Called by | BO-954, BO-956 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| seatMapId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |
| Prefer | header |  | enum (validate-only) | Validate, do not write (29 September, AI system design 2.3 and 2.2 D step 4). |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| addSections | array of object |  |  |
| addSections[].code | string | yes | (max length 20) |
| addSections[].name | string | yes | (max length 100) |
| addSections[].boundary | array of Point |  |  |
| addSections[].boundary[].x | number | yes |  |
| addSections[].boundary[].y | number | yes |  |
| addRows | array of object |  |  |
| addRows[].sectionCode | string | yes |  |
| addRows[].label | string | yes | (max length 10) |
| addRows[].seatCount | integer | yes | (min 1; max 500) |
| addRows[].firstSeatNumber | integer |  | (min 0; default 1) |
| addRows[].numberingDirection | enum (leftToRight, rightToLeft) |  | (default leftToRight) |
| addRows[].start | Point |  |  |
| addRows[].start.x | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].start.y | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].end | Point |  |  |
| addRows[].end.x | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].end.y | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].curveRadius | number |  | Null or absent for a straight row. (nullable) |
| addRows[].categoryId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| addSeats | array of object |  |  |
| addSeats[].sectionCode | string | yes |  |
| addSeats[].rowLabel | string | yes |  |
| addSeats[].seatNumber | string | yes |  |
| addSeats[].position | Point |  |  |
| addSeats[].position.x | number | yes |  |
| addSeats[].position.y | number | yes |  |
| addSeats[].categoryId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| addSeats[].attribute | SeatAttribute: enum (standard, accessible, companion, obstructedView, restrictedLegroom, premium, houseSeat, buffer, …) |  | BL-168. |
| removeSeatIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |

| relabel[].seatId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| relabel[].sectionCode | string |  |  |

| relabel[].seatNumber | string |  |  |

### setSeatRecommendationRules

**`PUT /seat-recommendation-rules`**: Scoring for best seat, best value, closest, family and accessible

Board 9, and it is the 21 August minute made configurable: *"best-seat ranking (e.g. last-row-is-best vs. first-row-is-best, depending on venue sightlines) must be configurable per seat map/event."*
**Five recommendation kinds, one scoring model.** *Best seat*, *best value*, *closest to stage*, *family together* and *accessible* are the same weighted score with different weights — and writing them as five algorithms guarantees they eventually contradict each other.
`recommendSeats` already exists and consumes this; it had no rules to consume.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `CAPACITY_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `seating.recommendation_rule` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Config scope | venue |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.recommendation_rule` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.recommendation_rule` |
| Called by | BO-1034, BO-1035, BO-1036, BO-1037, BO-1038, BO-1041 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `SeatRecommendationRules`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| seatMapId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| performanceId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| profiles | array of object |  |  |
| profiles[].kind | enum (bestSeat, bestValue, closestToStage, familyTogether, accessible, upgrade) |  |  |
| profiles[].weights | object |  | Sightline, distance, centrality, row, price, availability, aisle proximity, legroom. |
| profiles[].preferredSectionIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| profiles[].avoidSectionIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| profiles[].contiguityWeight | number |  | (nullable) |
| reverseRowOrder | boolean |  | Last-row-is-best against first-row-is-best, which the minute names as the worked example and which differs by venue sightline. (default False) |
| explainToGuest | boolean |  | (default True) |
| scopePath | string |  |  |

**Response**: `SeatRecommendationRules`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| seatMapId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| performanceId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| profiles | array of object |  |  |
| profiles[].kind | enum (bestSeat, bestValue, closestToStage, familyTogether, accessible, upgrade) |  |  |
| profiles[].weights | object |  | Sightline, distance, centrality, row, price, availability, aisle proximity, legroom. |
| profiles[].preferredSectionIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| profiles[].avoidSectionIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| profiles[].contiguityWeight | number |  | (nullable) |
| reverseRowOrder | boolean |  | Last-row-is-best against first-row-is-best, which the minute names as the worked example and which differs by venue sightline. (default False) |
| explainToGuest | boolean |  | (default True) |
| scopePath | string |  |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Set |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

## Tables

Every table this service owns that the slice reads or writes, with its columns as derived into `backend/tenant/*.sql`.

| scope_path | text | no | The partition key (ADR-0005). |
| id | uuid | yes | Synthesised key. |
